ISAAC — FULL LIST FOR EXPANDING THE UNLOCK CATALOG
Compiled on: 2026-10-15
Project: Skill Issue Tracker

Scope: all 641 secrets of the local Repentance / Repentance+ reference,
34 characters, all 717 visible Repentance collection items, 2 additional
utility active abilities, 188 trinkets, 114 consumable/group entries
and 45 challenges. The remaining existing graph nodes are listed separately.

This is an implementation task list, not the state of a player save.
[ ] means the entry still has to be verified and brought to a full implementation.
Having a dedicated node does not mean all of its conditions have been verified.
Names and source conditions are kept in the reference language for exact searching.
Collection snapshot: Repentance 1.7.9b.J835.
The list does not claim completeness for later updates, mods or other editions.

KEY NOTATION
- SECRET — a reference Save Secret ID; it must not be called a Steam API ID.
- collectible:N, trinket:N, consumable:N — separate game ID spaces.
- Characters are listed by stable tracker IDs; roster_index is not used as PlayerType.
- Some consumables have no numeric ID in the source. None has been invented for them.
- Pill groups are aggregated in the source reference: this is not a list of every effect.
  All related unlock conditions are still listed in the secrets section.
- Source conditions are material to verify, not a ready-made AND/OR schema.
- Secrets are used as reference mappings for content availability.
  Slot achievements are confirmed only by progress or an explicit manual fact.

SECTIONS
1. Main checklist of all secrets and unlock conditions: 1–637 + 638–641.
2. Characters: regular and Tainted.
3. Visible collection items: active, passive and item forms.
4. Additional utility abilities outside the collection.
5. Trinkets.
6. Cards, runes, souls, consumables and pill groups.
7. Challenges: access, completion and reward.
8. Other existing graph nodes: routes, bosses and mechanics.
9. Procedure for further additions, and sources.

========================================================================================
1. MAIN CHECKLIST — ALL 641 SECRETS / UNLOCKS
========================================================================================

6. CONSUMABLES — 114 ENTRIES, 97 OF THEM WITH A NUMERIC ID
========================================================================================

Cards, runes, souls and Cracked Key use consumable:N.
They are followed by 17 entries without a numeric ID: special pickups,
pills and aggregated groups. Confirm the type and game ID before adding them.

========================================================================================
9. HOW TO ADD ENTRIES FROM THIS LIST
========================================================================================

For each SECRET from section 1:
1. Verify the exact condition and game edition against game resources / sources.
2. Find existing nodes by ID and reuse shared dependencies.
3. Describe the AND/OR logic and a separate direct proof of the result.
4. Use completion marks, counters, completed flags, the collection and the bestiary.
   For actions that are not saved, keep an explicit manual confirmation.
5. Do not substitute a secret flag for meeting the condition.
6. Replace the corresponding manual Dead God item with a link to the shared node,
   keeping goalSecretId and exactly one coverage of each condition.
7. Prepare a local icon, an English description and Russian translation (site/js/locales/ru.js), and source links.
8. Run npm test, npm run validate:data, npm run build.
9. Mark [x] in this list after the implementation has been verified.

Rules for adding: .agents/skills/isaac-unlocks/SKILL.md
Schema: docs/unlock-schema.md
Repentance+ profile: site/js/catalog-profile.js

LOCAL SOURCES
- data/catalog.json — items, trinkets, consumables, characters, secrets, rewards.
- site/data/dead-god-collection.json — exact composition of the 717-item collection.
- data/challenges-repentance.json — 45 challenges and additional access conditions.
- site/data/unlocks.json — existing graph nodes and conditions.
- site/js/catalog-profile.js — four additional Repentance+ conditions.
